\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{|I|\vee|I_{p}|}+\frac{K}{|I|}\sum_{i_{1}\in I}\|r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r}\right)\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\setminus W}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{K}{|I_{p}|}\sum_{i_{2}\in I_{p}}\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\setminus W}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|I|\vee|I_{p}|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

If
r|k_{0}|c_{2}\leq\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}
, we have, using (
27
)

\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}\geq c_{2}|k_{0}||W|^{\frac{1}{d}}/|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}

Subsequently,

|I|\vee|I_{p}|\geq|I|\geq\left(c_{2}/\sqrt{2}\right)^{d}|W||k_{0}|^{d}.

If
r_{p}|k_{0}|c_{2}\leq\sqrt{2}|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}
, we have the same lower bound for
|I|\vee|I_{p}|
. Accordingly, the bound (
57
) is always valid.

Since we also have
|k_{j}+\tilde{k_{j}}|\geq c_{2}|k_{0}|
, the technique for bounding
B
can be applied to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I\\
i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle C\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|W||k_{0}|^{d}}+\mathcal{E}\right).

(58)

It remain to consider
D
. According to Lemma
D.2

\displaystyle|D|\leq K\|r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r}\right)\|_{4}^{2}\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{4}^{2}=K(rr_{p})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\|\psi_{i_{1}}\|_{4}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{2}}\|_{4}^{2}.

Using Lemma
F.3
and assumption (
27
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in I\times I_{p}}|D|\leq K\frac{(\log(|I|^{\frac{1}{d}})\log(|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{d}}))^{\frac{d}{2}}}{r^{\frac{d}{2}}|I|^{1/4}r_{p}^{\frac{d}{2}}|I_{p}|^{1/4}}\leq K\frac{(\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}.

(59)

Gathering the bounds (
56
), (
57
), (
58
) and (
59
), we obtain the result.
∎

To upper bound the variance of
\Delta_{I}
, we proceed in three steps with Lemmas
C.3
,
C.4
and
C.5
.

Lemma C.3
.

Suppose the assumptions of Theorem
6.1
hold and denote

\displaystyle C_{1}:=\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}))\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}})),

\displaystyle C_{2}:=\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}}))\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}})).

Then, we have, for
s\in\{1,2\}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq N\\
1\leq j_{2}\leq N\end{subarray}}|C_{s}|\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\left(\left(\frac{(|I_{p}|\vee|I|)^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right)+\frac{\log(|W|)^{2d}}{|W|^{2}}+\mathcal{E}(|I|,|I_{p}|)\right),

(60)

where
K<\infty
is a constant and
\zeta=d-1
.

Proof.

During the proof,
K
denotes a constant that may change from line to line.
We start by proving (
60
) for
s=1
. By bi-linearity, and using Corollary
G.1
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}))=\frac{1}{|I|^{2}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in I^{2}}

\displaystyle\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),\overline{C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}})},C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}),\overline{C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})})

\displaystyle+|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}))|^{2}+|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{1}_{i_{2}}(-k_{j_{2}}))|^{2},

(61)

where for
s\in\{1,2\}
,
C_{i}^{s}
denotes the quantity of equation (
9
) computed with
\Phi_{s}
. Using Lemma
D.2
, Lemma
F.3
and assumption (
27
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I|^{2}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in I^{2}}

\displaystyle|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),\overline{C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}})},C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}),\overline{C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})})|\leq K\frac{\log(|I|^{\frac{1}{d}})^{d}}{r^{d}|I|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}.

(62)

To bound the other terms, we will take benefit of the decorrelation between distinct frequencies. We start with the one involving
|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}))|^{2}
. Assume that
|k_{j_{1}}-k_{j_{2}}|r\geq\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}
. According to Lemma
D.4
, Lemma
F.1
and condition (
27
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I|^{2}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in I^{2}}|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}))|^{2}\leq K\mathcal{E},

On the opposite, if
|k_{j_{1}}-k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}
, then using Lemma
D.3
and Lemma
F.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I|^{2}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in I^{2}}|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}))|^{2}\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|I|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

We obtain the same upper bound for the term of (
C
) involving
|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{1}_{i_{1}}(-k_{j_{2}}))|^{2}
. Accordingly, we have

\displaystyle|\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}))|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}+\left(\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}-k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}+\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}+k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}\right)\frac{1}{|I|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

The same line of reasoning also applies to bound
\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))
, yielding

\displaystyle|\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}+\left(\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|r_{p}\leq\sqrt{2}|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}+\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}+\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|r_{p}\leq\sqrt{2}|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}\right)\frac{1}{|I_{p}|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

Subsequently, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}))\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{2d}}{|W|^{2}}+\delta+\mathcal{E}\right),

with

\displaystyle\delta=\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}\frac{1}{|I|N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\left(\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}-k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}+\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}+k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}\frac{1}{|I_{p}|N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\left(\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|r_{p}\leq\sqrt{2}|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}+\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}+\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|r_{p}\leq\sqrt{2}|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\left(\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}-k_{j_{2}}|\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\wedge\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}+\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}+k_{j_{2}}|r_{p}\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\wedge\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}\right)

\displaystyle=:\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}+\delta_{3}.

With the
k_{0}
-allowed assumption, we obtain

\displaystyle\delta_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}\frac{c_{3}}{N^{2}|I|}\left(N^{2}\left(\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+N\right)\leq K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I|}\left(\left(\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right).

Similar computations yield

\displaystyle\delta_{2}\leq K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I_{p}|}\left(\left(\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right),~\delta_{3}\leq K\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\left(\left(\frac{(|I_{p}|\wedge|I|)^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right).

Using the fact that
|I|\vee|I_{p}|\leq c_{5}|W|^{2\beta/(2\beta+d)}
, for upper bounding
\delta_{1}
and
\delta_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\delta\leq\frac{K}{|I||I_{p}|}\left(\left(\frac{(|I_{p}|\vee|I|)^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right).

This concludes the proof of (
60
) for
s=1
. The proof of (
60
) for
s=2
follows the same lines, but the details differs. By bi-linearity, and using Corollary
G.1
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))=\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I\\
i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),\overline{C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}})},C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}),\overline{C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})})

\displaystyle+|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}+|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{2}_{i_{2}}(-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}.

(63)

Using Lemma
D.2
, Lemma
F.3
and assumption (
27
), we obtain, as in (
62
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I\\
i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),\overline{C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}})},C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}),\overline{C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})})|\leq K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}.

Assume that
|k_{j_{1}}-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|\geq\sqrt{2}(|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}/r\vee|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}/r_{p})
. Using Lemma
D.4
and Lemma
F.1
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I\\
i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}~\leq K\mathcal{E}.

(64)

Suppose that
|k_{j_{1}}-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|\leq\sqrt{2}(|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}/r\vee|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}/r_{p})
. Using Lemma
D.3
and Lemma
F.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I\\
i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|I|\vee|I_{p}|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

(65)

We obtain a similar bound for the term involving
|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),C^{2}_{i_{2}}(-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}
, yielding

|\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}+\mathcal{E}\right)\\
+K\left(\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}+\mathds{1}_{|k_{j_{1}}+\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}\right)\frac{1}{|I|\vee|I_{p}|}.

By exchanging
j_{1}
and
j_{2}
, we have

|\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}))|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}+\mathcal{E}\right)\\
+K\left(\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}-k_{j_{2}}|\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}+\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}+k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}\right)\frac{1}{|I|\vee|I_{p}|}.

The previous inequalities gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}))|

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}+\mathcal{E}\right)^{2}+\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{2d}}{|W|^{2}}+\mathcal{E}+\delta\right),

where

\delta\leq\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|N^{2}|I|\vee|I_{p}|}\Bigg(\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}-k_{j_{2}}|\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}+\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}+k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}-k_{j_{1}}|\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}+\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}+k_{j_{1}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}\Bigg)\\
\qquad+\frac{1}{N^{2}(|I|\vee|I_{p}|)^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}-k_{j_{2}}|\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}+\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}+k_{j_{2}}|r\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{|I|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r}\vee\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}{r_{p}}}.

Using the
k_{0}
-allowed assumption, condition (
27
) and
|I|\leq c_{5}|W|^{2\beta/(2\beta+d)}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\delta|

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I|\vee|I_{p}|}+\frac{1}{(|I|\vee|I_{p}|)^{2}}\right)\left(\left(\frac{(|I|\vee|I_{p}|)^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{|I||I_{p}|}\left(\left(\frac{(|I_{p}|\vee|I|)^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right).

This ends the proof for the case
s=2
.
∎

Lemma C.4
.

Suppose the assumptions of Theorem
6.1
hold. Denote

\displaystyle B_{1}:=\mathbb{E}[\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}})-S_{\frac{1}{2}}(k_{j_{1}})]\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}})),

\displaystyle B_{2}:=\mathbb{E}[\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}})-S_{\frac{1}{2}}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}})]\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}})),

\displaystyle B_{3}:=\mathbb{E}[\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}})-S_{\frac{1}{2}}(k_{j_{2}})]\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}})),

\displaystyle B_{4}=\mathbb{E}[\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}})-S_{\frac{1}{2}}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}})]\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}})).

Then, for all
s\in\{1,4\}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|B_{s}|\leq K\left(b(I,I_{p})+b(I_{p},I)+b+\mathcal{E}(|I|,|I_{p}|)\right),

(66)

for a constant
K\in(0,\infty)
and where, for
\zeta=d-1
,

\displaystyle b(J_{1},J_{2}):=\left(\frac{|J_{1}|}{|W|}\right)^{\frac{\beta}{d}}\left(\frac{1}{|J_{1}||J_{2}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\left(\frac{|J_{2}|^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right)+\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||J_{1}|}\right),

\displaystyle b:=\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{2}|k_{0}|^{d}}+\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{\frac{5}{3}}}+\frac{1}{|k_{0}|^{2d}|W|^{2}}.

Proof.

We only need to prove the upper bound for
|B_{1}|
and
|B_{2}|
, since by exchanging
j_{1}
and
j_{2}
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|B_{3}|=\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|B_{1}|,~\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|B_{4}|=\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|B_{2}|.

We start with
B_{1}
. Using Lemmas
A.2
and
F.2
together with assumption (
27
) we have

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}})-S_{\frac{1}{2}}(k_{j_{1}})]|\leq K\frac{|I|^{\frac{\beta}{2d}}}{r^{\beta}}\leq K\left(\frac{|I|}{|W|}\right)^{\frac{\beta}{d}}.

For
s\in\{1,2\}
, we denote by
C_{i}^{s}
the quantity of equation (
9
) computed with
\Phi_{s}
. By multi-linearity, we have

\displaystyle\kappa(\widehat{S}_{0}

\displaystyle(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}}))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|I|||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{i_{2}\in I,(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}}\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})\overline{C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})},C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})\overline{C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})},C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})\overline{C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})}).

We denote

\displaystyle A_{1}:=C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),~A_{2}:=\overline{C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})},~A_{3}:=C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}),~A_{4}:=\overline{C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})},~A_{5}:=C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}),A_{6}:=\overline{C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})}.

Using Corollary
G.1
, we expand
\kappa(A_{1}A_{2},A_{3}A_{4},A_{5}A_{6})
:

\displaystyle\kappa(A_{1}A_{2},A_{3}A_{4},A_{5}A_{6})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma\in\Pi[6],\\
\sigma\vee\tau_{6}=1_{6}\end{subarray}}\prod_{b\in\sigma}\kappa(A_{s};s\in b),

where
\tau_{6}=12|34|56
and
1_{6}=123456
. Since the random variables considered are centered, the sum runs only over the partitions having blocks of size at least
2
. In the following, we use the notation

\displaystyle\kappa_{\pi}=\prod_{b\in\pi}\kappa(A_{s};s\in b).

In the sum over
\sigma
, 5 categories of partitions appear, denoted by
I
,
II
,
III
,
IV
and
V
. To fix ideas, the partitions involved are as follows (refer to
[
54
]
, Table 1):

\displaystyle\textbf{I:}

\displaystyle 123456

\displaystyle\textbf{II:}

\displaystyle 13|2456,\quad 14|2456,\quad 15|2346,\quad 16|2345,

\displaystyle 23|1456,\quad 24|1356,\quad 25|1346,\quad 26|1345,

\displaystyle 35|1246,\quad 36|1245,\quad 45|1236,\quad 46|1235

\displaystyle\textbf{III:}

\displaystyle 123|456,\quad 124|356,\quad 125|346,\quad 126|345,

\displaystyle 234|156,\quad 134|256

\displaystyle\textbf{IV:}

\displaystyle 135|246,\quad 235|146,\quad 145|236,\quad 136|245

\displaystyle\textbf{V:}

\displaystyle 13|25|46,\quad 13|26|45,\quad 14|25|36,\quad 14|26|35,

\displaystyle 15|23|46,\quad 15|24|36,\quad 16|23|45,\quad 16|24|35

We bound the cumulant of the partition of category
I
. According to Lemma
D.2
,

\displaystyle|\kappa_{123456}|

\displaystyle\leq K\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{6}^{2}\|r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r}\right)\|_{6}^{2}\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{3}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{6}^{2}=Kr_{p}^{-\frac{4d}{3}}r^{-\frac{2d}{3}}\|\psi_{i_{1}}\|_{6}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{2}}\|_{6}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{3}}\|_{6}^{2}.

Then, using Lemma
F.3
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I|||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{123456}|

\displaystyle\leq K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{r_{p}^{\frac{4d}{3}}|I_{p}|^{\frac{4}{9}}r^{\frac{2d}{3}}|I|^{\frac{2}{9}}}=K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}|I_{p}|^{\frac{2}{9}}|I|^{\frac{1}{9}}}{r_{p}^{\frac{4d}{3}}|I_{p}|^{\frac{2}{3}}r^{\frac{2d}{3}}|I|^{\frac{1}{3}}}

\displaystyle\leq K\frac{\log(|W|^{d})|W|^{1/3}}{|W|^{2}}=K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{\frac{5}{3}}},

where in the last line we used assumption (
27
).

For the partitions of class
II
, two types of terms appear, depending on either the block of size
2
contains a taper of index
i_{2}\in I
or not.
In the first case, the corresponding partitions are given by

\displaystyle\textbf{II}_{a}:=\{13|2456,~14|2456,~23|1456,~24|1356,35|1246,~36|1245,~45|1236,~46|1235\}.

We bound the term
35|1246
and the computations are similar for the others. By Lemma
D.2
, we have

\displaystyle|\kappa_{1246}|\leq K\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{4}^{2}\|r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r}\right)\|_{4}\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{3}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{4}.

(67)

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I|||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{35|1246}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|I_{p}|r_{p}^{\frac{d}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}\in I_{p}}\|\psi_{i_{1}}\|_{4}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}\frac{\|\psi_{i_{2}}\|_{4}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{3}}\|_{4}^{2}}{(rr_{p})^{\frac{d}{2}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})),C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}\sqrt{\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})),C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})|^{2}},

where in the last line, we used Lemma
F.3
and assumption (
27
). Using the fact that the family
(r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i}(\cdot/r)e^{-\bm{i}k_{j_{2}}\cdot})_{i\in\mathbb{N}^{d}}
is orthogonal, we have by Lemma
D.1

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})),C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})|^{2}\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|I|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

Accordingly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I|||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{35|1246}|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

By performing identical computations for the other partitions of category
\textbf{II}_{a}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\sigma\in\textbf{II}_{a}}|\kappa_{\sigma}|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

We now consider the case where the block of size
2
does not contain tapers of index
i_{2}
, i.e. the partitions

\displaystyle\textbf{II}_{b}:=\{15|2346,~16|2345~25|1346,~26|1345\}.

We detail the bound of the term
15|2346
. Using Lemma
D.2
, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Lemma
F.3
, we have

\displaystyle 2_{b}:=\frac{1}{|I|||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{15|2346}|

\displaystyle\leq K\frac{1}{|I|r^{\frac{d}{2}}}\sum_{i_{2}\in I}\|\psi_{i_{2}}\|_{4}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{|I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I_{p}\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}\frac{\|\psi_{i_{2}}\|_{4}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{3}}\|_{4}^{2}}{r_{p}^{d}}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{|I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I_{p}\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|}\sqrt{\frac{1}{|I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I_{p}\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}}.

Using (
64
) and (
65
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\in I_{p}\\
i_{3}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}\leq\frac{K}{|I_{p}|}\mathds{1}_{|\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|r_{p}\leq\sqrt{2}|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2d}}}+K\mathcal{E}.

This yields, using the
k_{0}
-allowed assumption:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}|2_{b}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{N}{N^{2}}\left(N\left(\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+1\right)+K\mathcal{E}

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\left(\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right)\right).

Now, we consider the partitions of categories

and

such that

We detail the computations for the partition

, that can be directly used for bounding the other terms. Using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{123|456}|\leq\\
\sum_{(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}}\sqrt{\sum_{i_{2}\in I}\left|\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\overline{C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})},C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}))\right|^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{i_{2}\in I}\left|\kappa(\overline{C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})},C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}),\overline{C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}})})\right|^{2}}.

Since
(r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i}(\cdot/r)e^{-\bm{i}k_{j_{2}}\cdot})_{i\in\mathbb{N}^{d}}
is orthogonal, we can apply Lemma
D.6
to get

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{2}\in I}\left|\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\overline{C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})},C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}))\right|^{2}\leq K\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{4}^{4}+K\delta,

with

\displaystyle\delta=|I|\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\setminus W}\|_{3}+\sum_{i\in I}\|r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r}\right)\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\setminus W}\|_{3}.

Using Lemma
F.1
and assumption (
27
), we get, after computations,

.
Subsequently,

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{123|456}|\leq K\sum_{(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}}\left(\frac{\|\psi_{i_{1}}\|_{4}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{3}}\|_{4}^{2}}{r_{p}^{d}}+\mathcal{E}\left(\frac{\|\psi_{i_{1}}\|_{4}^{2}}{r_{p}^{\frac{d}{2}}}+\frac{\|\psi_{i_{3}}\|_{4}^{2}}{r_{p}^{\frac{d}{2}}}+\mathcal{E}\right)\right).

Using Lemma
F.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{123|456}|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I|}+\mathcal{E}\right).

For the partitions of category

such that

\displaystyle\leq K\sup_{i\in I_{p}}\left(\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\setminus W}\|_{3}r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{6}}+\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\mathcal{F}[\psi_{i}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)]\mathds{1}_{|\cdot|\geq|\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}|/3}\|_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=K\sup_{i\in I_{p}}\left(\|\psi_{i}\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\setminus\frac{W}{r_{p}}}\|_{3}r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{3}}+\|\psi_{i}\mathds{1}_{|\cdot|\geq c_{1}r_{p}|k_{0}|/3}\|_{1}\right),

Using Lemma

On the opposite, assume that

. The Cauchy-Schwarz inequality yields

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{125|346}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\

Using Lemma

and Lemma

, we obtain after computations

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{125|346}|

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W||I_{p}|}+\mathcal{E}\right)\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{2}|k_{0}|^{d}}+\mathcal{E}\right),

since
|I_{p}|>\sqrt{2}|W||k_{0}|^{d}
. Subsequently, we have proved that in both cases

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{125|346}|\leq K\left(\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{2}|k_{0}|^{d}}+\frac{1}{|k_{0}|^{2d}|W|^{2}}+\mathcal{E}\right),

In the study of

, it remains to consider the partitions of category

(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{13|25|46}|\leq K\mathcal{E},

. Assume now that

. Using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{13|25|46}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(\overline{C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})},C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|\sqrt{\sum_{i_{2}\in I}|\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}},C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}})))|^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{i_{2}\in I}|\kappa(C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}},C^{1}_{i_{2}}(k_{j_{2}}))|^{2}}.

, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\
(i_{1},i_{3})\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa_{13|25|46}|

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(\overline{C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})},C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|

\displaystyle\leq K|I_{p}|\sqrt{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1},i_{2}\in I_{p}\end{subarray}}|\kappa(\overline{C^{2}_{i_{1}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}})},C^{2}_{i_{3}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|^{2}}\leq K|I_{p}|^{\frac{3}{2}}.

-allowed assumption, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|^{2}N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{2}\in I\\

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\left(\frac{|I_{p}|^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right)\right).

and

Lemma C.5
.

Suppose the assumptions of Theorem
6.1
hold. Then,

\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq N\\
1\leq j_{2}\leq N\end{subarray}}|\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}}))|\leq\\
K\left(\frac{1}{|I||I_{p}|}\left(\left(\frac{(|I|\vee|I_{p}|)^{\frac{1}{d}}}{|W|^{\frac{1}{d}}|k_{0}|}\right)^{\zeta}+\frac{1}{N}\right)+\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{\frac{5}{3}}}+\frac{1}{|k_{0}|^{2d}|W|^{2}}+\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{2}|k_{0}|^{d}}\right)+\mathcal{E},

for a constant
K\in(0,\infty)
and
\zeta=d-1
.

Proof.

We proceed as in the previous lemma. By multi-linearity and using Corollary
G.1
, we have

\displaystyle\kappa(\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{1}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{1}}}),\widehat{S}_{I}(k_{j_{2}}),\widehat{S}_{0}(\tilde{k_{j_{2}}}))=\frac{1}{|I|^{2}|I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(i_{1},i_{3})\in I^{2}\\
(i_{2},i_{4})\in I_{p}^{2}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma\in\Pi[8],\\
\sigma\vee\tau_{8}=1_{8}\end{subarray}}\prod_{b\in\sigma}\kappa(A_{s};s\in b),

where

\displaystyle A_{1}:=C^{1}_{i_{1}}(k_{j_{1}}),~A_{2}:=C^{1}_{i_{1}}(-k_{j_{1}}),~A_{3}:=C^{2}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),~A_{4}:=C^{2}_{i_{2}}(-\widetilde{k_{j_{1}}}),

\displaystyle A_{5}:=C^{1}_{i_{3}}(k_{j_{2}}),~A_{6}:=C^{1}_{i_{3}}(-k_{j_{2}}),~A_{7}:=C^{2}_{i_{4}}(\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}),~A_{8}:=C^{2}_{i_{4}}(-\widetilde{k_{j_{2}}}).

In the sum over the partitions, there are
12
categories that appear. To fix the idea, one representative of each of them is given by (refer to
[
54
]
, Table 1):

\displaystyle\textbf{I}~:~12345678~(1),\quad\textbf{II}~:123457|68~(24),\quad\textbf{III}~:12345|678~(24),\quad\textbf{IV}~:13578|246~(32),

\displaystyle\textbf{V}~:1235|4678~(24),~\textbf{VI}~:1357|2468~(8),\quad\textbf{VII}~:1235|47|68~(96),~\textbf{VIII}~:1357|24|68~(48)

\displaystyle\textbf{IX}~:123|457|68~(96),~\textbf{X}~:124|567|28~(48),~\textbf{XI}~:137|258|46~(96),~\textbf{XII}~:13|25|47|68~(48).

and the number of partitions inside each category is written between the parenthesis.
In the following, we use the notation

\displaystyle\kappa_{\textbf{C}}:=\frac{1}{|I|^{2}|I_{p}|^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(i_{1},i_{3})\in I^{2}\\
(i_{2},i_{4})\in I_{p}^{2}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\sigma\in\textbf{C}}\prod_{b\in\sigma}|\kappa(A_{s};s\in b)|,

where
C
is a category of partitions.
We start by bounding the partitions containing a block of size larger than 6. For the partitions of category
I
, we have, according to Lemma
D.2
,

\displaystyle|\kappa(A_{i};i\in[8])|

\displaystyle\leq K\|r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}(\frac{\cdot}{r})\|_{8}^{2}\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{8}^{2}\|r^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{3}}(\frac{\cdot}{r})\|_{8}^{2}\|r_{p}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{4}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r_{p}}\right)\|_{8}^{2}

\displaystyle=r^{-\frac{3d}{2}}\|\psi_{i_{1}}\|_{8}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{3}}\|_{8}^{2}~r_{p}^{-\frac{3d}{2}}\|\psi_{i_{2}}\|_{8}^{2}\|\psi_{i_{4}}\|_{8}^{2}.

Using Lemma
F.3
and assumption (
27
), we obtain

\displaystyle\kappa_{\textbf{I}}\leq K\frac{\log(|W|)^{d}}{r^{\frac{3d}{2}}|I|^{\frac{5}{12}}r_{p}^{\frac{3d}{2}}|I_{p}|^{\frac{5}{12}}}\leq\frac{K\log(|W|)^{d}(|I||I_{p}|)^{\frac{1}{3}}}{|W|^{3}}\leq\frac{K\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{\frac{7}{3}}}.

For the partitions of category
II
, we bound the cumulant of size two by a constant (using Lemma
D.1
) and using Lemma
D.2
, we obtain

\kappa_{\textbf{II}}\leq K\sup_{J_{s}}\frac{1}{|J_{1}||J_{2}|}\sum_{i_{1}\in J_{1},i_{2}\in J_{2}}\|r(J_{1})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{1})})\|_{6}\|r(J_{2})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{2})}\right)\|_{6}\\
\frac{1}{|J_{3}||J_{4}|}\sum_{i_{3}\in J_{3},i_{4}\in J_{4}}\|r(J_{3})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{3}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{3})})\|_{6}^{2}\|r(J_{4})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{4}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{4})}\right)\|_{6}^{2},

where
(J_{s})_{s\in[4]}
takes values in
\{I,I_{p}\}
and exactly two
J_{s}
are equal to
I
. Moreover,
r(I)=r
and
r(I_{p})=r_{p}
. Note that
J_{1}
and
J_{2}
are determined by the block of size
2
of the partitions. Using Lemma
F.3
and assumption (
27
), we get after computations

\displaystyle\kappa_{\textbf{II}}\leq K\sup_{J_{s}}\prod_{s\in\{1,2\}}\frac{\log(|W|)^{d/6}}{r(J_{s})^{\frac{d}{3}}|J_{s}|^{\frac{1}{9}}}\prod_{s\in\{3,4\}}\frac{\log(|W|)^{d/3}}{r(J_{s})^{\frac{2d}{3}}|J_{s}|^{\frac{2}{9}}}\leq\frac{K\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{\frac{5}{3}}}.

For the partitions of category
III
, the block of size
3
contains one index
i_{s}
,
s\in~[4]
appearing twice. Accordingly, using Lemma
D.2
, we bound them as

\kappa_{\textbf{III}}\leq K\sup_{J_{s}}\frac{1}{|J_{1}|}\sum_{i_{1}\in J_{1}}\|r(J_{1})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{1})})\|_{3}\|r(J_{1})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{1})})\|_{5}\frac{1}{|J_{2}|}\sum_{i_{2}\in J_{2}}\|r(J_{2})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{2}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{2})})\|_{3}^{2}\\
\frac{1}{|J_{3}||J_{4}|}\sum_{i_{3}\in J_{3},i_{4}\in J_{4}}\|r(J_{3})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{3}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(i_{3})})\|_{5}^{2}\|r(J_{4})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{4}}\left(\frac{\cdot}{r(i_{4})}\right)\|_{5}^{2},

where
(J_{s})_{s\in[4]}
takes values in
\{I,I_{p}\}
and exactly two
J_{s}
are equal to
I
. Moreover,
r(I)=r
and
r(I_{p})=r_{p}
. By Cauchy-Schwarz, we have

\frac{1}{|J_{1}|}\sum_{i_{1}\in J_{1}}\|r(J_{1})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{1})})\|_{3}\|r(J_{1})^{-\frac{d}{2}}\psi_{i_{1}}(\frac{\cdot}{r(J_{1})})\|_{5}\\

F.3
and assumption (

), we get after computations

For the partitions of category

, we bound them as

Gathering the previous bounds, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq N}\kappa_{\textbf{I}}+\dots+\kappa_{\textbf{VI}}\leq\frac{K\log(|W|)^{d}}{|W|^{\frac{5}{3}}}.

For the other categories of partitions, we rely on decorrelation between distinct frequencies. We start with the partitions of category

\displaystyle 1235|47|68,~1235|67|48,~1236|47|58,~1236|57|48,~1237|45|68,~1237|46|58,~1238|45|67
